# 1584d24 Add gitkb notes for the deployment tweak and Langfuse v4 upgrade commits

## Summary
Adds generated gitkb notes for the commits made after the initial knowledge build (the deploy fetch + reset --hard tweak and the Langfuse v4 upgrade), re-rendering the notes so the parent links and index stay consistent.

## Why
Keeps the knowledge base current after new commits, as the /gitkb workflow requires.

## Risks
Generated content only; none identified.
